Even at home, dishes and laundry are just piling up because I don't have the energy to keep up with anything anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any other changes, such as having trouble organizing your thoughts, speaking in a way that others find hard to follow, or experiencing a significant decline in your personal hygiene?\n\nPatient: My head feels stuffed with cotton, and I've had to reread a simple text from my sister several times just to understand it. I even freeze up at work because I can't remember what drink I was in the middle of making. On top of that, even taking a shower completely drains me, so it's been hard to keep up with basic hygiene.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any times where you have acted in a way that seemed odd or inappropriate to others, such as laughing for no reason or behaving in a way that didn't seem to fit the situation?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced anything like laughing for no reason, and my actions haven't felt inappropriate to me at all. When I confronted the owners, it felt completely justified given what they're doing to ruin my name, even if other people thought it was out of line.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way and dealing with these beliefs about the owners—has it been going on for less than six months, or has this been happening for a longer period of time?\n\nPatient: It’s been going on for about two months now, so definitely less than six months. It all started right around the time they cut my hours and promoted that younger bartender over me.\n\nDoctor: Did these symptoms, including your preoccupation with the owners and the difficulties with focus and hygiene, appear suddenly over a few days, or did they develop more gradually over these past two months?\n\nPatient: It was a gradual build-up over several months of stress before everything really spiraled these past two months. I first sank into a really dark, hopeless state where I felt completely drained of energy. The certainty that the owners were out to destroy my name only came after I was already in that dark place.\n\nDoctor: Besides the lack of energy and difficulty focusing, have you also been feeling persistently sad, hopeless, or empty, or have you lost interest in almost all the activities you used to enjoy?"

Even at home, dishes and laundry are just piling up because I don't have the energy to keep up with anything anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any other changes, such as having trouble organizing your thoughts, speaking in a way that others find hard to follow, or experiencing a significant decline in your personal hygiene?\n\nPatient: My head feels stuffed with cotton, and I've had to reread a simple text from my sister several times just to understand it. I even freeze up at work because I can't remember what drink I was in the middle of making. On top of that, even taking a shower completely drains me, so it's been hard to keep up with basic hygiene.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any times where you have acted in a way that seemed odd or inappropriate to others, such as laughing for no reason or behaving in a way that didn't seem to fit the situation?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced anything like laughing for no reason, and my actions haven't felt inappropriate to me at all. When I confronted the owners, it felt completely justified given what they're doing to ruin my name, even if other people thought it was out of line.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way and dealing with these beliefs about the owners—has it been going on for less than six months, or has this been happening for a longer period of time?\n\nPatient: It’s been going on for about two months now, so definitely less than six months. It all started right around the time they cut my hours and promoted that younger bartender over me.\n\nDoctor: Did these symptoms, including your preoccupation with the owners and the difficulties with focus and hygiene, appear suddenly over a few days, or did they develop more gradually over these past two months?\n\nPatient: It was a gradual build-up over several months of stress before everything really spiraled these past two months. I first sank into a really dark, hopeless state where I felt completely drained of energy. The certainty that the owners were out to destroy my name only came after I was already in that dark place.\n\nDoctor: Besides the lack of energy and difficulty focusing, have you also been feeling persistently sad, hopeless, or empty, or have you lost interest in almost all the activities you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yes, I've completely lost interest in everything I used to love, like bartending, photography, and trail running—it feels like all the color has been sucked out of my life. For the last couple of months, I've just been stuck in this dark, drained state where everything feels completely hopeless and pointless.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your belief about the owners trying to ruin you is directly related to your feelings of hopelessness and worthlessness, or does it feel like a separate issue that started on its own?\n\nPatient: They are definitely connected, because I only started being certain they were trying to ruin me after I had already sunk into this dark, hopeless state. Before these past two months of feeling deeply depressed, I never had these kinds of ideas or worries at all. It was only after everything felt pointless that I realized what they were doing behind my back."
